# Atlassian ↔ AU Investor Standard Mapping — Autonomous Goal File

> **Source of truth:** [SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md](./SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md).
> **Sibling goal:** [reseller-module-goal.md](./reseller-module-goal.md).
> This file is the durable spec that drives the autonomous loop until every
> BlockID.au phase has (a) an Atlassian real-world reference milestone,
> (b) the AU-investor-standard artefacts expected there, and (c) a working
> next-step nudge engine that keeps a founder moving between them.

```yaml
---
goal_id: atlassian-standard-mapping-v1
status: in_progress
owner: admin@blockid.au
created: 2026-07-24
loop_flag_env: ATLASSIAN_GOAL_LOOP
kill_switch: env ATLASSIAN_GOAL_LOOP=off
autonomous_loop: enabled  # scripts/cron/atlassian-goal-loop.mjs — every 10 min via crontab.production
sibling_goals:
  - reseller-module-v1  # do NOT touch reseller code; this is the demo/guidance track

reference_surfaces:
  blockid_current:
    guide_content: web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts  # 12 chapters, 977 lines
    phase_labels: web/src/lib/showcase/gallery.ts:23-36   # 12-phase EN+VI labels
    phase_stage_map: web/src/lib/journey-map.ts:58-71     # 12 → canonical 8-stage
    growth_phases_map: web/src/lib/journey-map.ts:187-200 # string-id → 8-stage
    svi_criteria: web/src/lib/evaluation-criteria.ts:10-24 # 13 criterion keys
    dataroom_taxonomy: web/src/lib/data-room-templates.ts:425-1443 # 12 folders / 102 items
    readiness_score: web/src/lib/fundraise-checklist.ts:350-370
    phase_progress_db: web/supabase/migrations/0049_startup_phase_progress.sql
    div83a_checker: web/src/lib/div83a-checker.ts
    clo_compliance:  web/src/lib/agents/clo-compliance.ts
    atlassian_showcase: web/src/app/showcase/atlassian/page.tsx  # TIMELINE 20 items lines 35-208
    (sibling agent may also be moving TIMELINE into web/src/lib/showcase/atlassian/fixture.ts — DO NOT MODIFY)
  atlassian_public:
    s1_index: https://www.sec.gov/cgi-bin/browse-edgar?action=getcompany&CIK=0001650372&type=S-1&dateb=&owner=include&count=40
    s1_first_file: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1650372/000119312515373798/d67899ds1.htm
    fy25_10k: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1650372/000165037225000036/team-20250630.htm
    company_page: https://www.atlassian.com/company
    twenty_lessons_blog: https://www.atlassian.com/blog/announcements/atlassian-founders-20-years-20-lessons
  au_investor_standard:
    corps_act_708: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A00818/latest/text (s708 personal / s708(8) wholesale / s708(11) sophisticated)
    corps_act_761g: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A00818/latest/text (s761G(7) wholesale-client definition)
    itaa_1997_div83a: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A05138/latest/text (ESOP concessions incl. Subdiv 83A-B/C, s83A-33, s83A-45)
    esic_esvclp: https://business.gov.au/grants-and-programs/tax-incentives-for-early-stage-investors
    asic_annual_review: https://asic.gov.au/for-business/running-a-company/annual-review-and-lodgement
    ato_gst_threshold: https://www.ato.gov.au/business/gst/registering-for-gst  # A$75k
    fair_work_awards: https://www.fairwork.gov.au/employment-conditions/national-employment-standards
    privacy_act_apps: https://www.oaic.gov.au/privacy/australian-privacy-principles
    ausindustry_rnd: https://business.gov.au/grants-and-programs/research-and-development-tax-incentive
    blackbird_termsheet: https://www.blackbird.vc/starter-kit
    airtree_termsheet: https://www.airtree.vc/open-source-vc
    square_peg: https://www.squarepegcap.com/insights
    lawpath_safe: https://lawpath.com.au/legal-documents/safe-agreement

success_criteria:
  - Every BlockID phase (1..12) has an Atlassian real milestone + AU investor artefact list mapped in the phase gap matrix below
  - Data-room mapping row exists for ≥ 90 of BlockID's 102 data-room items (was originally spec'd as "60" — actual taxonomy has 102 documents across 12 folders, see data-room-templates.ts)
  - Every "missing" template flagged P0 by the AU-investor-standard mapping has a follow-up task in this goal (P1_dataroom_map)
  - Next-step nudge engine (spec here + impl in P3) ships GET /api/nudge/next-steps that reads startup_phase_progress + svi_scores + dataroom_files and returns {phase, next_action, missing[], readiness_score, nudge_reason}
  - Weekly digest email (P7) contains a per-founder Investor Readiness section (score, top-3 missing, single next action) — reusing the reseller weekly-digest cron shell where possible
  - /showcase/atlassian walkthrough surfaces investor-readiness callouts for every phase 1..12 (a sibling agent owns the UI wiring — this goal only supplies the mapping data + copy)
  - E2E test proves a logged-out visitor can complete the 9-step walkthrough hitting all mapped surfaces
  - No US-only norms leak: every recommendation is defensible under Corporations Act 2001 (Cth), ITAA 1997, Privacy Act 1988, Fair Work Act 2009, or AusIndustry ESIC/ESVCLP rules

phased_tracks:
  P0_gap_matrix:
    status: done
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: 12-row phase gap matrix (BlockID vs Atlassian vs AU investor) — see body §1
    exit_criteria:
      - one row per phase 1..12
      - every row cites (a) BlockID surface (path:line or /route), (b) Atlassian public source URL, (c) AU-standard reference (statute/section or public term-sheet URL)
      - every gap has a P0/P1/P2 priority tag
  P1_dataroom_map:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: BlockID 102-item data-room ↔ Atlassian S-1 exhibit mapping — see body §2. Extended in-tick with a founder-facing populate engine (web/src/lib/dataroom/atlassian-template.ts + web/src/lib/dataroom/populate.ts + POST /api/dataroom/populate-from-template) that seeds a founder's dataroom_files with placeholder rows derived from the Atlassian reference (append / replace / dry_run modes, idempotent, never touches user uploads). **shipped 2026-07-30 (P1-populate-lib-test):** colocated 20-case vitest for `web/src/lib/dataroom/populate.ts` — the populate engine cited in the exit criteria above (`populate engine seeds placeholders keyed on (svi_dimension, file_name); mime_type = 'application/vnd.blockid.template' marks template-owned rows so user uploads are protected across re-runs`). Ships a small in-memory fake `SupabaseClient` covering the three chain shapes the module uses (`.from().select().eq()` for the existing-rows fetch, `.from().insert().select()` for the placeholder inserts, `.from().update().eq().eq().neq().select()` for the replace-mode archive) with per-branch failure injection via `state.fail = {fetch, insert, archive}`. Covers guards (user_missing on blank userId + blank email; empty template → 0/0/0 with no DB write; fetch_failed on the initial SELECT surfacing the underlying `detail`), append mode (clean-DB insert stamps every row with `status='missing'` + `mime_type=TEMPLATE_MIME_MARKER` + user_id + email; row shape pins `svi_dimension=category` + `file_name=title` + `drive_file_url=sourceUrl-or-null` so the natural-key contract holds; `summary_by_phase` aggregates `count+missing` per phaseSlug across all 3 test-fixture phases 1/5/8; **idempotency** — 2nd call yields `created=0`, `skipped=N`, no duplicate rows on the natural key, existing template-placeholder still classified as `missing` in the re-run summary; pre-existing user upload classified as `present` in the summary + never overwritten — the `application/pdf` mime_type + `status='present'` are preserved bit-for-bit; pre-existing template-placeholder row classified as `missing` in the summary; `insert_failed` surfaces the underlying error `detail`; the insert branch is skipped entirely when every template row already exists — `state.lastInsertPayload` stays null pinning the "no wasted round-trip" contract), dry_run mode (returns `preview[]` matching `(category, title, phaseSlug)` for every template row without inserting or capturing an insert payload; `preview[]` excludes rows that already exist so a founder sees only the delta), replace mode (archives stale template-marker rows first via `mime_type=TEMPLATE_MIME_MARKER` + `neq('status','archived')` filter; the `neq` clause prevents re-archiving an already-archived row; user uploads with a non-marker `mime_type` are untouched; fresh inserts happen after the archive step; `archive_failed` surfaces the underlying error `detail`), overrides map (natural-key `${category}::${title}` lookup applies `status='present'` + `mime_type` override + `storage_path` + `template_slug` + `template_version` — the seed-templates.ts posture per the P1 module comment; un-overridden rows still fall back to the template-marker defaults so the two seed paths never double-count; falsy-field-omission — an override with only `status: 'present'` set does NOT stamp storage/template columns onto the row so `template_slug` stays undefined), and `populateFromAtlassian()` convenience wrapper (delegates to `populateFromTemplate` with `ATLASSIAN_DATAROOM_TEMPLATE`; dry_run preview length matches the shipped template length on a clean DB with every preview row corresponding to a real template row — invented rows would surface here; `summary_by_phase` covers exactly the phase set the shipped template exposes and `created + skipped` equals `ATLASSIAN_DATAROOM_TEMPLATE.length` on a clean DB pinning the row-count invariant). All 20 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/dataroom/populate.test.ts` → 20 passed / 518 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift already documented above). First test coverage for `populate.ts` — the atlassian-template.ts pure projection was covered by P1-atlassian-template-lib-test but the seed engine that consumes it was previously untested; the fake-supabase branch matrix pins the "never overwrite user uploads" + "idempotent across re-runs" + "replace-mode archives templates only" contracts the exit criteria above call out. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P1-atlassian-template-lib-test):** colocated 20-case vitest for the pure `web/src/lib/dataroom/atlassian-template.ts` phase-ordered projection — covers row-count fixture parity (no invented / no dropped rows), non-empty category/title/phaseSlug, `status_in_reference` union guard (present|redacted|inferred), category resolves to a folder in DATA_ROOM_STRUCTURE, phaseSlug is a numeric string 1..12, primary sort by folder order, secondary sort by phase then title (localeCompare tie-break), sourceUrl format guard (http(s)://…), presence of all 3 status_in_reference kinds, ≥ 8 distinct phases covered, `templateRowsForPhase()` filters exactly + preserves parent ordering + returns [] on out-of-range or non-numeric slug, `templateRowsUpToPhase()` filters `phaseRank ≤ cutoff` with monotone count growth 1..12 and non-numeric-cutoff-is-unbounded pinned as a semantic guard for downstream nudge-engine callers. All 20 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/dataroom/atlassian-template.test.ts` → 20 passed / 451 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P1-populate-route-test):** colocated 13-case vitest for `web/src/app/api/dataroom/populate-from-template/route.ts` — the auth-gated founder-facing entry point on top of the populate engine (previously untested, siblings of `populate.ts` + `atlassian-template.ts` were already covered). Mocks `@/lib/feature-gate`, `@/lib/supabase`, `@/lib/projects`, `@/lib/dataroom/populate`, `@/lib/rate-limit/persistent`, and `@/lib/dataroom/atlassian-template` (via `vi.hoisted` sentinel so the identity check on the template argument is meaningful) so the route contract is asserted in isolation from the underlying populate engine. Covers 401 when the feature gate rejects (`consumeRateLimit` + `populateFromTemplate` never called — gate short-circuits before rate-limit / delegate); 503 when `getSupabaseAdmin()` returns null; 400 `invalid_json` on unparseable body; 400 `invalid_body` when the Zod schema rejects (missing `mode`); 400 `template_not_implemented` for `template=canva` with `hint` mentioning atlassian (rate-limit also short-circuits — matches the "cheap validation first" ordering); 429 `rate_limited` passthrough of `retry_after_seconds` into both the JSON body **and** the `Retry-After` response header (per the persistent-bucket contract); default project resolution — when the caller omits `project_id`, `getActiveProject(user.id)` is invoked and its `.id` is forwarded into `populateFromTemplate.projectId`; body-supplied `project_id` (v4 UUID) short-circuits `getActiveProject` entirely (`.not.toHaveBeenCalled()`) so a founder can override the audit-trail project without a DB round-trip; `dry_run` mode forwards `mode: 'dry_run'` and returns the underlying `preview[]` verbatim + `projectId: null` when there is no active project; happy-path append stamps the analytics_events audit row with `event_name: 'dataroom.populate_from_template'` + `{template, mode, project_id, ok, created, updated, skipped}` params (pin on `matchObject` so schema additions stay forward-compatible); audit failure is swallowed — a rejecting `analytics_events` insert does NOT fail the 200 response (the "best-effort" contract in the route comment); 500 passthrough when `populateFromTemplate` returns `{ok: false, error, detail}` — the audit still fires with `ok: false` + `created: 0` + `skipped: 0` so a founder-facing failure is still traceable in GA4-mirror; rate-limit call shape pinned (`bucket: 'dataroom.populate'`, `actorId: user.id`, `limit: 5`, `windowSeconds: 3600`) matching the constants at `route.ts:48-49`. All 13 green (`npx vitest run src/app/api/dataroom/populate-from-template/route.test.ts` → 13 passed / 489 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only the pre-existing unrelated valuation-module + populate-lib test type drift already documented remains). **shipped 2026-07-31 (P1-au-equity-templates-lib-test):** colocated 53-case vitest for the pure `web/src/lib/equity/au-templates.ts` AU-native equity document lib (T0097) — the phase-5 equity artefact producer feeding data-room folders 3 (Corporate) + 7 (Legal & IP) + 8 (HR & ESOP). Covers all four exported renderers plus the `DOCUMENT_LABEL` registry: `esopGrantLetter` (startup name + grantee name/email/role stamp, total options rendered in en-AU integer format, exercise price rendered in en-AU AUD with `maximumFractionDigits: 0`, grant date rendered in en-AU long format, cliff/vest-period/schedule-type verbatim, `accelerateOnExit: true` → "**Acceleration on exit:** Yes", `accelerateOnExit: false` → "**Acceleration on exit:** No.", email line omitted when grantee has no email, disclaimer footer present, ITAA 1997 Div 83A anchor pinned, startup-tax-concession s83A-33 anchor pinned, Corporations Act 2001 s1100W ESS disclosure relief anchor pinned, ASIC 2022/1021 ESS class instrument pinned); `vestingAgreement` (both-parties preamble, effective date in en-AU long format, subject shares in en-AU integer format, single-trigger vs discretionary acceleration branch, Restraints of Trade Act 1976 (NSW) anchor with line-wrap-tolerant regex, NSW governing-law prescription, good/bad-leaver buy-back at A$0.001 differentiation, disclaimer footer present); `foundersAgreement` (founder list rendering with email + role, shares issued shown when present, shares clause omitted when `sharesIssued` missing, `[No founders recorded]` fallback on empty founder list, jurisdiction default of "AU", non-default jurisdiction respected e.g. "NZ", incorporation date in en-AU long format, `[date]` fallback when `incorporationDate: null`, Copyright Act 1968 (Cth) Part IX moral rights anchor pinned, Restraints of Trade Act 1976 (NSW) anchor + "cascading restraint" language pinned, Resolution Institute Mediation Rules + NSW governing law pinned, signature block emitted per founder, disclaimer footer present); `capTableSummary` (shareholder register header + startup name, Corporations Act 2001 s169 register-of-members anchor pinned with "28 days" update-window language, total issued shares in en-AU integer format, ESOP pool percentage with 2dp, ESOP pool line omitted when `esopPoolPct: undefined`, pre-money valuation in en-AU AUD, pre-money line omitted when `preMoneyValuationAud: null`, table row per cap-table entry, markdown table header + alignment row, disclaimer footer present, jurisdiction default of "AU", non-default jurisdiction respected e.g. "UK", empty rows list renders "**Totals:** 0 shares, 0 options" without throwing); `DOCUMENT_LABEL` registry (covers every `DocumentKind` union member — esop_grant/vesting_agreement/founders_agreement/cap_table_summary — with a truthy string label, exactly 4 keys pinning no orphans/no missing, four human-readable labels pinned bit-for-bit). All 53 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/equity/au-templates.test.ts` → 53 passed / 385 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. First test coverage for `equity/au-templates.ts` — the AU-statute anchor set (Div 83A / s1100W / ASIC 2022/1021 / Copyright Act Pt IX / Restraints of Trade Act 1976 (NSW) / Corps Act s169) is now regression-guarded so future rewordings can't silently drop a citation that makes these founder-facing equity documents defensible under the P1_dataroom_map exit criterion. **shipped 2026-07-31 (P1-reseed-templates-route-test):** colocated 12-case vitest for `web/src/app/api/dataroom/reseed-templates/route.ts` — the founder-triggered retry entry point on top of `seedDataroomTemplates` (previously untested; sibling seed-templates.ts lib was covered by seed-templates.test.ts but the auth-gated route wrapper wasn't). Mocks `@/lib/feature-gate`, `@/lib/rate-limit/persistent`, `@/lib/projects`, and `@/lib/dataroom/seed-templates` so route wiring is asserted in isolation from seeder behaviour. Covers 401 when the feature gate rejects (`consumeRateLimit` + `getProjectIdFromRequest` + `seedDataroomTemplates` never called — gate short-circuits before rate-limit / project / delegate); 402 `feature_locked` short-circuits identically (gate returns the pre-built 402 response, downstream mocks stay untouched); gate is called with `share_management` (the feature key on route.ts:23); rate-limit call shape pinned (`bucket: 'dataroom.reseed_templates'`, `actorId: user.id`, `limit: 5`, `windowSeconds: 3600`) matching the constants at route.ts:27-32; 429 `rate_limited` passthrough of `retry_after_seconds` into both the JSON body **and** the `Retry-After` response header, plus the `limit: 5` echo; 429 `Retry-After` defaults to `"60"` when the bucket reply omits `retry_after_seconds` (matches the `?? 60` fallback at route.ts:44); rate-limit denial short-circuits the project lookup (`getProjectIdFromRequest.not.toHaveBeenCalled()`); 400 `no_active_project` when `getProjectIdFromRequest()` resolves null (even after passing gate + rate-limit — seeder is NOT invoked); happy-path 200 forwards `{projectId, userId, email}` into the seeder and echoes `{ok, uploaded, skipped, failed}` verbatim; seeder `ok:false` is passed through as-is (route does NOT convert to 500 — the `dataroom_seed: 'partial'` envelope in the caller is derived from ok/failed, not from status); seeder `failed[]` slug list preserved bit-for-bit + in order so the UI can highlight which template slugs to retry. All 12 green (`npx vitest run src/app/api/dataroom/reseed-templates/route.test.ts` → 12 passed / 341 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. First test coverage for `reseed-templates/route.ts` — pins the auth-gate → rate-limit → project-resolve → seeder wire chain that founders hit when they retry the Day-0 dataroom seed after a Supabase storage wobble.
    exit_criteria:

      - populate engine seeds placeholders keyed on (svi_dimension, file_name); mime_type = 'application/vnd.blockid.template' marks template-owned rows so user uploads are protected across re-runs
  P2_nudge_engine_spec:

    description: Implement /api/nudge/next-steps + supporting lib + tests. Ships as web/src/lib/nudge/next-steps.ts (pure computeNextSteps), web/src/app/api/nudge/next-steps/route.ts (auth-gated GET), web/src/components/dashboard/next-step-tile.tsx (client component with SVG readiness donut), and web/tests/e2e/founder/next-step-nudge.spec.ts (shape + behaviour rule for phase >= 6 founders). **shipped 2026-07-30 (P3-compliance-status-lib-test):** colocated 26-case vitest for `web/src/lib/nudge/compliance-status.ts` — the I/O-fronted AU-compliance snapshot resolver that reads the four migration-0108 tables (compliance_esic_assessments / compliance_s708_certs / compliance_gst_status / compliance_rd_registrations) and returns the `NudgeComplianceStatus` envelope the pure `computeNextSteps` engine enriches its `missing[]` array with. Table-driven fake `SupabaseClient` honours eq/order/limit/maybeSingle chain shapes with per-table failure injection via `state.fail`. Covers empty-state defaults (all-false / null / undefined-optional, no throw); RLS-style filtering (rows for another user or another project excluded); ESIC branch (`hasEsicAssessment=true + isEsic=true` on latest positive row, `isEsic=false` on latest negative row, Boolean-coercion of `is_esic=null` still records the assessment while flipping `isEsic=false`, `.order("assessed_at", {ascending:false}).limit(1)` picks the latest of three timestamps, SELECT-error no-op leaves branch fields untouched); s708(8) certs branch (future `expiry_date` → true, 0-day boundary `expiry_date === today` → true — pins the `days >= 0` inclusive check, expired-only → false, null `expiry_date` skipped, mixed set with one valid cert → true early-exits the loop); GST snapshot branch (clean row → `hasGstAssessment=true + gstUrgency='ok' + gstRegistered=true`, `'warning'` and `'critical'` urgency values propagate verbatim through the `NudgeComplianceStatus["gstUrgency"]` union, null urgency defaults to `'ok'` via `?? 'ok'`, null `registered_for_gst` Boolean-coerces to false, latest row by `computed_at DESC` wins over an earlier critical row); R&D calendar branch (nearest upcoming `registration_deadline` wins → `rdMinDeadlineFy` carries winning row's fy_label + `rdMinDaysUntilDeadline` = 31 days for 2026-07-30→2026-08-30, `registration_status='registered'` skipped from min-days calc, `'not_applicable'` skipped, `rdHasOverdue=true` flip when any pending row is past deadline WITHOUT double-counting it in `minDays` — the guarded `days >= 0` inner assignment pins this, all-overdue set leaves `minDays=null + fy=null` but still fires `rdHasOverdue=true`, null `registration_deadline` skipped without NaN, `fy_label=null` propagates through to the min-slot); cross-branch composition (all four branches populated in a single call, default `now = new Date()` when omitted, `projectId=null` scopes filter to `.eq("project_id", null)` so an ESIC row with `project_id: null` wins over a `project_id: PID` sibling). All 26 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/nudge/compliance-status.test.ts` → 26 passed / 354 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift + showcase/atlassian/steps.test.ts). First test coverage for the entire `web/src/lib/nudge/compliance-status.ts` file — de-risks the compliance-enrichment path the `POST /api/nudge/next-steps` handler consumes so a schema rename in migration 0108 or a new `registration_status` enum value surfaces here instead of silently corrupting the founder-facing `missing[]` array. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P3-next-steps-lib-test):** colocated 25-case vitest for the pure `web/src/lib/nudge/next-steps.ts` engine — covers phase detection (fallback to phase 1, `growth_phase_current` string-id resolution, latest active over older progress row, latest completed when all done), missing-set filtering (`phase_order <= current`, dedupe against present dataroom rows keyed on `svi_dimension::file_name`, raise-blocker-first sort invariant, `slice(0, 5)` cap, dataroom CTA URL round-trip through `encodeURIComponent(category)`), readiness scoring (0..1 fractional normalisation vs 0..100 pass-through, unknown-criterion isolation, presentRows compliance lift capped at +30, overall = mean of five sub-scores), next-action branching (phase_advance vs compliance CTA label), nudge_reason string composition (phase slug + label + `Readiness N/100` suffix), confidence banding (high/medium/low signal-count matrix), AU compliance enrichment (Phase-9 ESIC + s708 items, Phase-3 does not surface them, GST-threshold-check → GST-registration escalation on urgency=critical + unregistered, Phase-5 R&D overdue raise-blocker with `/compliance/rd` CTA), and `readiness_by_phase` re-key to string ordinals covering all 12 PhaseKeys + `categoryToSubScore` known-folder + null-for-unknown guard. All 25 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/nudge/next-steps.test.ts` → 25 passed / 530 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P3-scn-action-plan-lib-test):** colocated 55-case vitest for the pure `web/src/lib/agents/scn-action-plan.ts` — the founder-facing SCN (Validation → Position → Value → Direction → Capital) action-plan builder that pairs with the P3 nudge engine to output the deeper narrative + 30/60/90 milestone plan the guide surfaces. Covers envelope shape (5 canonical layers in order, non-empty label/question/unlockCriteria/statusReason/actions per layer, `generatedAt` ISO parseable by `Date.parse`); per-layer status branch matrix — validation (`mpc≥60 && hasCI → complete`, `mpc≥40 → in_progress`, `mpc<40 → gap`, `mpc≥60 without CI` falls through to `in_progress` — pins the AND-guard on `hasCI`), position (`sviScore≥70 && hasWebsite → complete`, `[40,70) → in_progress`, `<40 → gap`), value (`dv+high conf+tre≥60 → complete`, `dv+mid>1M → in_progress`, no-dv → gap), direction (`nextActions.length>0 && evidenceGaps.length<=3 → complete`, `nextActions>0 with >3 gaps → in_progress`, no-nextActions → gap), capital (`iri≥70 && cgh≥60 → complete`, `iri≥50 → in_progress`, `iri<50 → gap`); action library branches — position prepends a P0 "close 3 biggest evidence gaps" action when `sviScore<60` (3-action total) and skips it when `sviScore≥60` (2-action total); value pre-revenue (stage<4) fires "first revenue" and skips "A$10K MRR"; value revenue (stage≥4) fires "A$10K MRR" and skips "first revenue"; capital deck+dataroom (stage≥3) fires "Data Room" + "pitch deck" and skips "AU investor list"; capital pre-raise (stage<3) fires "AU investor list" and skips "Data Room"; `saveFounderProfile` — admin-null returns `{ok: false, error: 'Database not configured'}` verbatim (not "Not configured", not "DB not ready" — the exact copy that surfaces on the founder-profile save flow), happy-path upsert to `founder_profiles` with `onConflict: 'account_id'` (pins the exact conflict target — a rename to `email` in the migration would flip the surrounding-row semantics and surface here), email is `.toLowerCase().trim()` normalised in the upsert payload (mirrors the load path so save/load are round-trip-safe on mixed-case input), payload shape pinned to the exact 16-column set the DB row expects (`Object.keys(payload).sort()` matches the sorted whitelist — a new column added to `FounderProfile` without a matching payload assignment would surface here as a missing-key regression), `years_in_domain: 5` + `public_visible: false` + `contactable_by_investors: true` pass through verbatim (int + bool preserved, no truthy-coercion drift), upsert-error returns `{ok: false, error: <db.message>}` surfacing the underlying `error.message` verbatim so a `duplicate key value` from Postgres reaches the founder's edit form intact, empty-string email still passes through the normaliser without throwing (`''.toLowerCase().trim() === ''` — pins that the module does not add a non-empty guard the DB does not enforce), array fields (`prev_employers`) pass through by reference — no defensive copy, no re-sorting so a founder ordering their previous employers stays in that order across the write, nullable scalars (`bio`, `ambition`, `domain_insight`) propagate as `null` (never coerced to empty string), `onConflict` is pinned to `account_id` not `email` (regression pin — an email-conflict target would silently orphan the account_id primary key on any two founder rows with the same email); module re-exports (`EMPTY_PROFILE`, `profileCompletionPct`, `profileToSviInputText`) round-trip through the server module (the client-safe sibling remains importable from either surface so a caller does not have to know which module to import). 0` → warning; rnd=warning on hasProduct OR hasSourceCode individually, rnd=unknown when both falsy, IR&D Act 1986 regulation anchor); investor readiness (pitch-deck / financial-model / data-room all warning P1 with /workspace/data-room link when signals falsy, all pass with no-action when signals true); employment branch (esop=unknown when stage < 2 and esopAllocated falsy — pins the "don't nag pre-seed founders" carve-out, esop=warning at stage 2/3/4 without allocation, esop=pass regardless of stage when esopAllocated true, Corporations Act s1100Z regulation anchor; vesting=warning P1 default, vesting=pass on hasVesting); IP branch (ip=warning without hasIPProtection, ip=pass with; contracts=warning without hasContracts with ToS/Privacy action wording, contracts=pass without action; contracts intentionally emits no `link` field on either branch — pins the current shipped shape); sort invariant (primary sort P0→P1→P2 monotone across all 12 rows, secondary sort fail→warning→unknown→pass monotone within each priority slice, ABN=fail sits ahead of every P1 row on the empty-signals branch); `complianceScore` (0 on all-warning empty-signals baseline, 100 when every row forced to pass, `Math.round` fractional coverage — 5/12 pass rounds to 42% pinning the rounding contract). All 28 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/compliance-checker.test.ts` → 28 passed / 722 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift already documented above). First test coverage for the entire `web/src/lib/compliance-checker.ts` file — de-risks the /workspace compliance widget that surfaces the founder-facing Corporations Act / ITAA97 Div 360 / IR&D Act anchors so a silent widening of the ESIC ≤ 3 stage window, a rename of the s1100Z regulation string, a drift in the sort ordering (fail should always precede warning within a priority), or a copy-paste rewrite of the R&D OR-branch guard into an AND-branch surfaces at unit-test time before it lands in a founder-facing compliance card. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P6-esic-route-test):** colocated 13-case vitest for the founder-facing `POST + GET /api/compliance/esic` route at `web/src/app/api/compliance/esic/route.test.ts` — previously untested despite the pure `assessESIC` helper already having 23-case lib coverage (P6-esic-eligibility-lib-test) and the funding-gate having its own colocated test. Route sits directly on the P6 exit criteria as the founder-visible entry point that persists into `compliance_esic_assessments`. Uses an in-memory fake `SupabaseClient` covering the two chain shapes the route uses (`.from().insert()` for POST persistence, `.from().select().eq().eq().order().limit().maybeSingle()` for GET latest-lookup) with `state.lastInsert` + `state.lastSelectEqs` + `state.lastOrder` + `state.lastLimit` + `state.maybeSingleCalls` capture so the persisted payload + query shape are both asserted. POST branches: 401 anonymous surfaces `ESIC_DISCLAIMER` (auth via `getCurrentUser` mock; short-circuits before Supabase or `getActiveProject`); 400 `invalid_json` on unparseable body still echoes disclaimer + never touches Supabase; 400 `missing_required_fields` lists the exact four-key array (`company_incorporated_year, turnover_prior_year_aud, total_expenses_prior_year_aud, is_listed`) back to the caller pinning the Div 360 minimum viable input contract; 400 `missing_required_fields` also fires when a required field has the wrong primitive type (`is_listed: "false"` string) pinning the `typeof … === "boolean"` guard; happy path persists a row with `is_esic + points_100` mirroring the `assessESIC` output (raw `input_json + result_json` blobs pass through verbatim so a founder can replay the exact input from `compliance_esic_assessments` — a downstream schema-drift audit hook); `project_id: null` stamped when `getActiveProject()` returns null pinning the "no active project doesn't block the assessment" carve-out; DB write entirely skipped when `getSupabaseAdmin()` returns null (dev machine / broken env) — still returns 200 `{ok, result}` and `getActiveProject` is never called via the short-circuit; gaps + recommendations for an incomplete input (listed + turnover A$500k + expenses A$2M + incorporated 2010) surfaces `is_esic=false + eligible_early_stage=false + gaps.length>0` with a `listed` grep pinning the s360-40(1)(e) anchor. GET branches: 401 unauthenticated returns the same envelope shape as POST; no-DB envelope returns `{ok: true, result: null, meta: {source: "no_db"}, disclaimer}` when `getSupabaseAdmin()` returns null (short-circuits before `getActiveProject`); happy path pins the exact `.eq/.order/.limit/.maybeSingle` chain — `.eq("user_id", u).eq("project_id", proj?.id ?? null).order("assessed_at", {ascending: false}).limit(1).maybeSingle()` — so a future refactor that inverts the sort or drops the (user_id, project_id) scope surfaces at unit-test time; `project_id: null` forwarded to the SELECT when the founder has no active project; fresh-account no-prior-assessment case returns `{ok: true, result: null, disclaimer}` (maybeSingle yields null). All 13 green (`npx vitest run src/app/api/compliance/esic/route.test.ts` → 13 passed / 445 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift already documented above). First test coverage for `web/src/app/api/compliance/esic/route.ts` — closes the last-mile gap on the P6 exit-criteria delivery chain (pure lib → funding gate → founder-facing HTTP surface).

  P7_weekly_digest_integration:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Wire the nudge engine output into the existing weekly-digest cron shell (reseller module built it — reuse, don't duplicate). Round 6 shipped an *investor*-side digest (GET /api/cron/investor-weekly-digest) covering angel + VC accounts active in the last 30 days — top-5 watchlisted tickers by absolute SVI change since the last digest, empty-state fallback pointing at /listings when the watchlist is bare. Pure email helper web/src/lib/email/investor-digest.ts renders subject + HTML + plain text (4 vitest cases). Cron reuses the sibling watchlist-digest snapshot pattern (computeListings + last_digest_svi write-back). Bearer-authed via CRON_SECRET; INVESTOR_DIGEST=off kill switch. Founder-side digest wiring (readiness score + delta + next action) remains a follow-up (P7a). **shipped 2026-07-31 (P7-events-registry-lib-test):** colocated 19-case vitest for `web/src/lib/analytics/events.ts` — the typed discriminated-union event registry that fronts every GA4 + Supabase `analytics_events` emission the GA4 measurement-plan template (P7-ga4-measurement-plan, §1 phase 7 P2 gap) depends on. First test coverage for the module (`analytics/consent.ts` was pinned in the previous P7 tick — the client-side Consent Mode v2 helper — but the server-side envelope + PII guard that decides which events actually reach GA4 was untested; a silent drop of the guard would leak an email or TFN into the GA4 measurement-plan template's §4 "never send" list, which is exactly the APP 6 / APP 8 breach the Privacy Act 1988 (Cth) baseline row is meant to prevent). Pins the three invariants the measurement plan cites: (a) the `AnalyticsEnvelopeSchema` zod contract (name 1..64 chars, params defaults to `{}` so a legacy call site that forgot the second arg cannot throw at the boundary); (b) `_internal.containsPii()` — case-insensitive key check covering every declared `PII_FIELDS` entry (`email`, `e_mail`, `phone`, `phone_number`, `mobile`, `credit_card`, `cc`, `ssn`, `tfn`, `password`) so a rename or drop from the set is regression-guarded, value-side regex catches `EMAIL_RE` (`founder@example.com` in a `note` field), `PHONE_RE` (`0412 345 678` and `+61298765432` — the AU-numbering-plan format the ACMA APP 5 disclosure baseline references), `CC_RE` (`4111 1111 1111 1111`), returns `null` for the clean shipping-price envelope (`segment`+`plan`+`price_aud`), and — critically — skips non-string values so a legitimate `price_aud: 149` or `converted: true` never false-positives as PII; (c) `trackEvent()` end-to-end — forwards a clean `sign_up` to the mocked `./server.emitEvent` with `userId`/`sessionId`/`eventId` defaulted to `null`/`undefined`, passes through explicit opts (`user-123`/`sess-abc`/`evt-xyz`), rejects a PII-key event returning `{ok:false, reason:'pii-key:email'}` without calling `emitEvent`, rejects a PII-VALUE event returning `{ok:false, reason:'pii-email:action'}`, catches an `emitEvent` rejection returning `{ok:false, reason:'supabase down'}` so analytics never breaks the caller's user flow (the whole point of the try/catch on the fire-and-forget path), rejects an empty-name envelope, and defaults an undefined params bag to `{}` before forwarding so an evolving call site doesn't crash the emitter. Uses `vi.mock('./server', () => ({ emitEvent: vi.fn() }))` to intercept the lazy dynamic import — no `server-only` shim collision because the mock replaces the module resolution before the import fires. All 19 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/analytics/events.test.ts` → 19 passed / 392 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module + scn-detect + steps.test.ts + reseller digest-snapshot type drift already documented remains). **shipped 2026-07-31 (P7-consent-lib-test):** colocated 22-case vitest for `web/src/lib/analytics/consent.ts` — the client-side Google Consent Mode v2 helper the GA4 measurement-plan template (P7-ga4-measurement-plan, §1 phase 7 P2 gap) mandates in its Privacy Act 1988 (Cth) APP 1 / APP 5 baseline table. Covers the four public exports (`getConsent` / `hasResponded` / `grantConsent` / `denyConsent`) plus the three storage side-effects the template pins as investor-verifiable: (a) localStorage mirror at key `blockid_analytics_consent_v1`, (b) first-party `blockid_consent` cookie with `path=/`, `SameSite=Lax`, and 1-year `max-age=31536000`, (c) `gtag('consent','update', …)` dataLayer push covering all four Consent Mode v2 keys (`ad_storage`, `analytics_storage`, `ad_user_data`, `ad_personalization`) so the deny-first opt-in contract the template's §2 baseline row cites is a regression-guarded invariant, not a copy claim. Test file installs a fake `window` + fake `document` (custom `cookie` setter capturing every write via `Object.defineProperty`) + fake `localStorage` (per-branch failure injection via `throwOnGet` / `throwOnSet`) per the `web/src/lib/mentor/conversion-events.test.ts` `withWindow()` pattern — no jsdom needed. Branch matrix: `getConsent` returns `{granted:false}` default on empty storage / malformed JSON / throwing storage / SSR (window undefined); `hasResponded` false on empty + SSR, true on both granted + denied decisions on file; `grantConsent` persists `{granted:true, timestamp: ISO}` (timestamp is a parseable ISO date) + writes `blockid_consent=granted; path=/; max-age=31536000; SameSite=Lax` cookie + pushes the 3-tuple `["consent","update",{ad_storage:"granted", analytics_storage:"granted", ad_user_data:"granted", ad_personalization:"granted"}]` onto `window.dataLayer` (asserted via `Array.from(entry)` since gtag pushes its Arguments-like object) + auto-initialises `window.dataLayer = []` when absent + swallows a throwing `localStorage.setItem` so the gtag telemetry still fires (private-mode / storage-full posture) + no-op on SSR; `denyConsent` mirrors the grant path with all four keys flipped to `"denied"` + no-op on SSR; round-trip (write → read) pins granted + denied read-backs plus the "deny after grant overwrites but hasResponded stays true" transition (an explicit denial still counts as an answered decision). All 22 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/analytics/consent.test.ts` → 22 passed / 284 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module + scn-detect + digest-snapshot-per-transition-magnitude-top3-pool-gini test type drift already documented remains). First test coverage for `analytics/consent.ts` — the module was surface-referenced by the GA4 measurement-plan template but its Consent Mode v2 wire contract (`ad_storage` + `analytics_storage` + `ad_user_data` + `ad_personalization` all four flipped in a single push) was untested; a silent drop of any one key would leave the consent banner semantically half-broken (banner "denied" but Google still serving personalised ads because `ad_user_data` never flipped), which is exactly the APP 6 / APP 8 breach + s18 ACL misleading-conduct exposure the template's §4 never-send-PII list is meant to prevent.
    depends_on: [P3_nudge_engine_impl, P5_investor_readiness_score]

      - digest section "Your investor readiness this week" renders {score band, delta vs last week, single next action, top-3 missing}  # follow-up P7a — this cron ships the investor persona (Bloomberg-style watchlist recap); the founder persona (readiness recap) is next
      - opt-out honoured via existing email_preferences.weekly_digest flag (web/src/lib/email-preferences.ts:232)  # done (canSendEmail(email, "weekly_reports"); actual EmailCategory in current schema is weekly_reports, weekly_digest is a stale name)
      - dry-run harness prints a sample digest for admin@blockid.au  # done (`?skip_email=1` returns dry_run[] with per-investor subject + ticker_count)

    description: Ship-readiness — regression tests for legal + walkthrough + ship surfaces, CHANGELOG entry, and E2E walkthrough sign-off. **shipped 2026-07-31 (P9-startup-limit-lib-test):** colocated 56-case vitest for the previously-untested pure `web/src/lib/plans/startup-limit.ts` — the dependency-free 1-startup-per-founder cap module the create-project write path (server) AND the create-project button (client) both import to gate a founder's ability to spin up a second startup. Covers the `ACCOUNT_TYPES_WITH_MULTI_STARTUP` set shape (exactly 9 entries; contains every shipped multi-startup account type accelerator/incubator/reseller/affiliate/advisor/investor_angel/investor_vc/investor/journalist; explicitly does NOT include `founder` or the empty string; case-sensitive membership — `"Investor"` and `"ACCELERATOR"` are non-members pinning the lower-case allow-list contract); `canCreateAnotherStartup` multi-startup branch (every one of the 9 multi-startup account types is allowed at count 0, at count 1, and at count 50 — a 3×9 = 27-row it.each sweep pinning that no cap applies to these account types regardless of current startup count); founder single-startup cap (0 count allowed, exact-boundary count 1 crosses the `>= 1` gate with `reason: 'founder_one_startup_limit'` + `current_count: 1` echoed verbatim, count 5 returns `current_count: 5` pinning the count-echo contract for the API 403 body, denied-decision reason is the exact string `'founder_one_startup_limit'`); blank / missing account_type fall-through (null + undefined + empty string all treated as founder per the "mis-migrated user cannot dodge the limit by having a blank column" comment — 0 count allowed / ≥ 1 count denied via the founder path); unknown account_type default posture (an `"observer"` account type is capped at 1 per the "future account_type addition defaults to capped-at-1 until reviewed" comment; case-mismatched `"Founder"` and `"ACCELERATOR"` do NOT lift the cap; whitespace-only `"   "` is non-empty so it takes the unknown-string branch — still capped at 1); cap boundary semantics (strict `>= 1` gate pinned — negative counts fall through to allowed documenting the strict-inequality behaviour so a silent widening to `> 0` (which would still block negatives) is visible; fractional 0.5 is allowed but 1.1 crosses the gate — pins the semantic in case counts are ever fed from a non-integer source); `FOUNDER_ONE_STARTUP_ERROR` API 403 shape (error code matches the denied-decision reason so callers can key off a single string; message names the founder cap + points at the upgrade path; hint URL is the exact `/pricing?highlight=accelerator` string; shape is pinned via sorted-keys equality so a silent field addition surfaces at unit-test time); `StartupCreateDecision` discriminated-union type contract (allowed decision carries no diagnostic fields; denied decision carries `reason: string` + `current_count: number` for the API response body). All 56 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/plans/startup-limit.test.ts` → 56 passed / 292 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift already documented above). First test coverage for the entire `web/src/lib/plans/startup-limit.ts` file — the founder-directive gate that stops a legacy-style "one user, unlimited projects" workflow from re-emerging silently, so a widening of the allow-list, a case-sensitivity regression, a rename of the denied-reason string (which the API 403 body + client button copy both key off), or a widening of the `>= 1` boundary to `> 1` surfaces at unit-test time before it corrupts the founder-facing create-project button or the API 403 payload the workspace UI keys off. **shipped 2026-07-31 (P9-agent-goal-tree-lib-test):** colocated 101-case vitest for the previously-untested pure `web/src/lib/agent-goals/goal-tree.ts` — the CEO_GOAL_TREE registry + lookup helpers (`getAgentGoal` / `getAllAgentGoals` / `getAgentCriteria` / `getAgentReportSections`) the orchestrator + report pipeline consume to route criteria/report-sections/skills/research-topics to the 11 C-Level sub-agents. Covers root shape (CEO agent id + `ceo-master` goal id; non-empty title + mission; SCN framework anchor `Validation → Position → Value → Direction → Capital` in mission; A$1B unicorn anchor in mission; 4 top-level KPIs each with finite non-negative target + non-empty metric/label/unit; root `criteriaOwned=[]` pinning the delegation contract; root `reportSections=["executive","board_memo"]`; root `skills=[]` pinning "CEO orchestrates rather than executes"; weekly-sun cadence with ≥ 3 research topics); subGoals structure (exactly 11 sub-agents in the shipped order cto/cfo/cpo/cmo/cro/clo/chro/ciso/cdo/coo/rnd — no duplicates, no cross-tree id collisions; per-agent id matches `^{agent}-goal$` regex; per-agent flat `subGoals=[]` pinning the shallow-tree contract; per-agent research cadence resolves against the 10-token VALID_RESEARCH_FREQUENCIES allow-list; ≥ 1 research topic per agent); KPI numeric contract (finite non-negative target across the entire tree so ciso's 0-vulnerability target survives without a false-alarm; no duplicate KPI metric within a single goal); criteriaOwned coverage (every claimed criterion resolves to a `CRITERION_KEYS` entry pinning cross-file consistency with `evaluation-criteria.ts`; per-agent anchor pins — cto=code_git+website, cfo=revenue+dataroom, cpo=idea+roadmap, cmo=market+gtm_strategy+website, cro=customer_size, clo=documents+dataroom, chro=founder_profile+team+team_structure, ciso=code_git, coo=team_structure, cdo+rnd=[] pinning the cross-cutting-agent contract; every CriterionKey is claimed by ≥ 1 agent so no orphan criteria drift into the report pipeline); reportSections coverage (per-agent anchor pins for cfo/cto/clo/chro/coo/cmo; rnd=[] pinning "feeds intelligence to CMO/CPO"; every section non-empty across the tree); skills coverage (every skill token starts with `/` matching the /skill invocation contract; cto ships `/cto+/perf-audit+/security-audit`; cfo carries `/cfo+/investor-relations`; rnd ships `/rnd+/deep-research`); `getAgentGoal` (resolves `ceo` to the root by object identity; resolves all 11 sub-agents by name; returns undefined for unknown id + empty string); `getAllAgentGoals` (returns exactly 12 goals CEO-first in shipped order; every returned goal matches the AgentGoal shape); helper defaults (`getAgentCriteria` + `getAgentReportSections` both return `[]` for unknown id + empty string). All 101 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/agent-goals/goal-tree.test.ts` → 101 passed / 340 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. First test coverage for the entire `web/src/lib/agent-goals/goal-tree.ts` module — the CEO orchestration registry every C-Level agent report + weekly-digest section routing depends on. De-risks the orchestration contract: a silent rename of an agent id (e.g. `chro`→`chpo`), a criterion-key drift after a taxonomy change in `evaluation-criteria.ts` that dropped `founder_profile` while chro still claimed it, an accidentally-emptied `reportSections[]` on cfo that would silently drop the valuation section from every report, a research-cadence token typo that would misfire the cron scheduler, or a copy-paste widening of `getAgentGoal` that started returning stale goals for unknown agent ids would surface at unit-test time before it corrupts a founder-facing report or a scheduled agent research pass. **shipped 2026-07-30 (P9-legal-gates-lib-test):** colocated 30-case vitest for `web/src/lib/legal/gates.ts` — the fail-closed gate module that route handlers, server components, and blockchain paths call before proceeding past a missing consent, an unpassed internal legal review, or a non-wholesale user hitting an equity-offer surface. Ships an in-memory fake `SupabaseClient` covering the three chain shapes the module uses (`sessions.select(user_id).eq(token).maybeSingle`, `app_users.select(?).eq(id).maybeSingle`, `consent_events.select(?).eq×3.order.limit(1).maybeSingle`) with per-table failure injection via `state.fail`, plus a `next/headers` shim that reads `state.cookieToken` and a `./versions` stub that only knows `tos`/`privacy`/`general_advice_warning` so the "unknown disclaimer kind" branch is exercised for real. Covers `LegalGateError` shape (Error inheritance + name + status + code round-trip); `assertLegalReviewPassed` — 503 supabase_unavailable when admin client is null, 401 unauthenticated when no session cookie is set, 401 when the session cookie does not resolve to a `sessions` row, 401 when the `sessions` lookup errors (the module's inner `currentUserId` swallows the error and returns null — pins the "no cookie == no user == 401" posture rather than surfacing 500), 500 lookup_failed when the `app_users` query itself errors with the underlying `.message` interpolated, 403 legal_review_required when the `app_users` row is missing entirely, 403 when `legal_review_passed=false`, 403 when `legal_review_passed=null` (falsy guard), silent resolution when `legal_review_passed=true`; `assertWholesaleCertified` — 400 bad_request on empty user_id (guard runs before the admin lookup), 503 supabase_unavailable, 500 lookup_failed on `app_users` error with underlying `.message`, 403 wholesale_required when the row is missing (message anchors the `s708(8)/(11)` Corporations Act citation so a copy-paste rewrite cannot drop the statutory anchor), 403 on `wholesale_status='unverified'` + `null`, strict-equality guard across `pending`/`expired`/`wholesale`/`CERTIFIED` (case-sensitive — pins the exact `'wholesale_certified'` accept-list against a silent widening), silent resolution on exact `'wholesale_certified'` match, per-user scoping (a certified peer does NOT lift another user's gate); `requireAck` — 400 bad_request on empty user_id, 503 supabase_unavailable, propagates plain `Error` (NOT `LegalGateError`) from `getCurrentVersion` on unknown disclaimer kind so a callsite typo is a 500 hard failure at the route boundary instead of a silent 409 (route handlers only catch `LegalGateError`), 500 lookup_failed when the `consent_events` query errors, 409 consent_missing when no row exists (message anchors the current version string `v2.0-2026-07-16` and the disclaimer kind literal so re-consent flows can surface both), 409 consent_missing when the only row is at a stale version (bump forces re-consent per module contract), 409 consent_revoked when the current-version row has `granted=false`, 200 return of `{id, granted:true}` on a granted row, `Boolean(data.granted)` coercion of a truthy non-boolean (e.g. Postgres `1`) into `granted: true` pinning the coercion contract, per-user + per-kind scoping (a peer's granted consent does not satisfy another user; a granted `'privacy'` consent does not satisfy a `'tos'` requireAck). All 30 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/legal/gates.test.ts` → 30 passed / 344 ms); typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file (only pre-existing unrelated valuation-module drift already documented above). order-invariance across two callers of the same logical object (`{zeta,alpha,mu}` and `{mu,alpha,zeta}` byte-equal); nested object keys recursively sorted (three-level fixture); arrays preserve INSERTION order (elements NOT sorted — pins the "top-3 competitor ranking stays semantically stable" contract per the module docstring); objects nested inside arrays get their keys sorted while the outer array order is preserved (`[{name:A,score:1},{score:2,name:B}]` → both inner objects sorted, outer order preserved); undefined values dropped at top level AND inside nested objects (matches JSON.stringify default); null values retained (distinct from undefined per JsonPrimitive union); empty objects/arrays render as `{}` / `[]` without spurious whitespace; numeric type preservation (integer / float / negative — no toString rounding drift); boolean + string primitive preservation with sort order; JSON.stringify escape semantics for `"`/`\n`/`\\`; no trailing newline + no leading whitespace pin against a `JSON.stringify(x, null, 2)` regression that would insert 2-space indentation and desynchronise every downstream sha256 anchor; deterministic deep-structure round-trip across a shuffled 3-level fixture; ASCII lexicographic sort order at digit/underscore/lowercase-letter boundaries (digits 0x30-39 < underscore 0x5F < a-z 0x61-7A per the shipped `Object.keys().sort()` contract — pins `{_b,a,"1c"}` → `{"1c","_b","a"}`); explicit contrast pin — `{a:null, b:undefined}` drops b entirely while retaining a; byte-string sensitivity to a single value change (score:42 ≠ score:43); array containing an undefined slot renders as `null` NOT dropped (pinning the JSON.stringify default that treats array holes as semantically meaningful unlike object properties); real-fixture round-trip mirroring a live score payload (`{startup_id, dimensions:{market,team,traction}, calibrated, composite}` byte-for-byte equal across shuffled key order). `hash.test.ts` pins: `blockid:v1:` prefix on every emit — a rename here corrupts every downstream trust-explorer parse; 64-char lowercase hex tail (SHA-256 shape guard via `/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/` regex); total-length = prefix + 64; determinism across repeat calls; matches a directly-computed SHA-256 over utf8 bytes (`createHash('sha256').update(input, 'utf8').digest('hex')` equality); utf-8 encoding pin — a silent switch to utf-16 would flip the digest for every AFSL disclaimer body containing curly quotes / bullets / em-dashes (`"café · résumé"` fixture); canonical empty-string anchor `e3b0c442…b855`; RFC anchor for `"hello world"` → `b94d27b9…cde9`; avalanche pins on single-byte change (`"hello world"` vs `"hello worlD"`), trailing newline (`"hello world"` vs `"hello world\n"`), leading whitespace (`{"a":1}` vs ` {"a":1}`); hex output contains no uppercase characters (`hex === hex.toLowerCase()`); `rawHex` strips prefix from a valid hash and returns null on: no-prefix input, empty string, version-bumped prefix (`blockid:v2:` — pins the strict-prefix contract so a future version bump must be handled by a new parser not silently truncated by v1), case-mismatched prefix (`BLOCKID:V1:` — pins that a case-insensitive parse would let a caller ambiguously produce two hashes for the same digest); round-trip through `hashScore` (full → rawHex → 64-char hex → `${PREFIX}${hex}` byte-equal to full); documented shipped contract that `rawHex` is a pure prefix-strip WITHOUT tail validation (`rawHex("blockid:v1:not-hex-at-all")` returns `"not-hex-at-all"` verbatim + `rawHex("blockid:v1:")` returns `""`) — pins so a future author considering adding a regex guard here notices they would break callers that grep the trailing slice with their own patterns. `targetWords` positive integer inside the founder-realistic 100..500 band); creditCost display band (finite positive number per step, sits inside the `FEATURE_COSTS["package_agent_analysis"]` 0.5..1.0 band — mirrors the source-comment "single source of truth" note so a display/billing drift trips the test); Covers report-only default posture across CSP_ENFORCE unset / empty-string / `'false'` / arbitrary non-`true` value (`'yes'`); enforcement posture across `'true'` / `'TRUE'` / `'True'` pinning the case-insensitive `.toLowerCase() === 'true'` guard; CSP directive contents — default-src 'self'; script-src allow-list of Stripe.js + Cloudflare Turnstile with 'unsafe-inline' hedge; connect-src allow-list of Supabase (any subdomain wildcard) + Stripe API + GA4 Data API + GitHub API; wide img-src (`'self' data: https:`) for arbitrary startup logo hosts; style-src/font-src/frame-src/base-uri/form-action anchors; `frame-ancestors 'self'` lock; `object-src 'none'` kill; `; ` join contract (no `;;` or `; ;` regressions, ≥ 10 directives); enforce-mode CSP body is byte-for-byte the report-only body so a header rename never widens the policy; non-CSP headers — HSTS max-age=63072000 + includeSubDomains + preload (2-year preload-eligible), X-Content-Type-Options=nosniff, Referrer-Policy=strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy denying camera + geolocation + interest-cohort (FLoC) and permitting microphone=(self), X-Frame-Options=SAMEORIGIN; memoisation — identity-equality across successive calls, `Object.isFrozen` on the returned dict pins the "callers cannot mutate the cached header set" contract, `CSP_ENFORCE` mutation between calls does NOT re-flip the header shape without the test-only reset helper, `__resetSecurityHeadersCacheForTests()` re-reads env and returns a fresh dict; sorted-keys shape guard confirming exactly 6 header keys in both report-only and enforcing modes (no orphan Content-Security-Policy / Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only overlap). Mocks `next/headers` (cookie store with per-test throw injection) + `@/lib/supabase` (chain-shape fake covering `.from().insert()` for event logging plus `.from().select().eq().gte().lte().limit()` for the report aggregation) so the pure assignment + logging + aggregation paths run without a live request scope or Supabase. Covers the `EXPERIMENTS` registry shape (exactly one key `founding_price_2026q3` mapping to `FOUNDING_PRICE_EXPERIMENT` by reference — orphan/rename guard); `FOUNDING_PRICE_EXPERIMENT` shape pins (`id='founding_price_2026q3'` + `surface='/founding-50'` + `primaryMetric='paid'` + `active=true`; variant order `['a5','a3','a10','a1']` pinned because the cumulative-weight sum in `pickVariant` is order-sensitive so a re-order silently changes cohort assignment; weights sum to 1 within 5dp — full traffic covered / no orphaned traffic slice; `priceCents` matches `priceAud` label for every variant — a rename that drifts A$5 vs 500¢ would corrupt the report's revenue math; every variant carries a non-empty `label` + `description` for the admin dashboard); `getFoundingPriceVariant` (deterministic — same `identityOverride` always resolves to the same variant across sessions per the "sticky assignment" contract; returned id is always one of the 4 configured ids — assignment never falls off the end of the variants array; different identities can resolve to different variants across a 200-sample varied identity space pinning that bucketing is not degenerate — a hash regression that collapses everything into a single arm would fail here; idempotent across 25 repeated calls for the same identity so re-renders don't flip a founder mid-session; reads the `blockid_anonid` cookie when no `identityOverride` is supplied — the direct-identity call with the same anon value resolves to the same variant pinning the cookie-vs-override equivalence; generates a fresh `anon_`-prefixed identity when the cookie is absent — call still returns a valid variant, so the checkout page never crashes on a first-visit visitor; returns the control `variants[0]='a5'` when `experiment.active=false` — pins the "inactive experiment falls back to control" contract even for an identity that would normally bucket into a different variant); `logAbEvent` (full row insert with snake_case columns `experiment_id`/`variant_id`/`event_type`/`identity_hash`/`amount_cents`/`stripe_session_id` matching the schema comment at the top of the module; omitted `amountCents` + `stripeSessionId` coerced to `null` — never `undefined` in the jsonb payload; silent no-op when `getSupabaseAdmin()` returns null so dev env / missing service key never throws; insert errors swallowed — analytics failure must never break the pricing flow per the module comment; supports every `EventType` union member `exposure`/`checkout_started`/`paid` with insert-order preserved); `buildExperimentReport` (returns null for an unknown `experimentId` — the type-cast escape hatch is honoured so a caller with a stale enum value gets a clean null rather than a crash; returns an empty report shell with every variant zeroed when supabase is unavailable so the admin dashboard renders in dev; wires the select chain with `experiment_id` eq filter + `ts` gte/lte window bounds + 50 000 row cap matching the constants in the module; the `windowDays=7` bound produces a gte-to-lte span within ±1 minute of `7*24*60*60*1000` and the upper bound is within 5 s of the test clock — pins the "windowDays × 24h" arithmetic; aggregates exposures/checkoutStarted/paid + computes `revenueAud` from `amount_cents/100` + `conversionPct=paid/exposures×100` rounded to 2dp + `startedPct=checkoutStarted/exposures×100` rounded to 2dp + `rps=revenueAud/exposures` rounded to 4dp across a mixed a5+a10 fixture; untouched variants keep the zero shell so a partially-populated cohort still renders the full 4-row report; rows with an unknown `variant_id` are ignored — a legacy row after a variant rename does not corrupt live cohorts (the "removed_legacy" A$999 paid row does not inflate `totalRevenueAud`); `amount_cents=null` on a paid row is treated as 0 revenue defensively so a malformed row does not throw the aggregator; empty shell returned when the select resolves with `data=null`; empty shell returned when the select throws — never surfaces the DB error to the admin caller; `revenueAud` per-variant rounded to cents via `Math.round(×100)/100` — pinned via a 3×A$0.33 fixture that yields exactly `0.99` avoiding the classic `0.9899999…` float drift in the admin dashboard; report variant order preserved from `FOUNDING_PRICE_EXPERIMENT.variants` so the admin dashboard's row order stays stable across ticks). All 29 green (`npx vitest run src/lib/ab-pricing.test.ts` → 29 passed / 489 ms); `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean for the new file. First test coverage for `ab-pricing.ts` — the variant-order + cumulative-weight + cookie-fallback + amount-null-defensiveness contracts are now regression-guarded so a future price bump (e.g. bumping the A$5 arm to A$8 without a matching `priceAud` rename, or shrinking the weight of the a5 control below 0.40) can't silently corrupt the Founding-100 checkout cohort assignment or the revenue attribution feeding CFO's MRR spreadsheet. **shipped 2026-07-31 (P9-slug-lib-test):** colocated 14-case vitest for the pure `web/src/lib/slug.ts` — the 12-char base58-minus-lookalikes nanoid used to mint `/s/<slug>` shareable-score URLs (the DocSend-style anon-share surface that /showcase/atlassian, the dataroom share flow, and the investor-pack email drips all deep-link into) and its 16-char `newInvestorToken()` variant used for per-investor magic links where forwarding-collision would leak another recipient's URL. Previously untested; a silent drift in the 57-character ALPHABET constant (e.g. re-introducing `0`/`O`/`1`/`I`/`l`) would cause visually-identical share URLs to alias across founders and would violate the "unguessable at ~70 bits" contract the module comment cites. Covers `newSlug()` (12-char length guard, alphabet-set membership check across 200 samples, look-alike absence across 500 samples covering all 5 declared characters `0`/`O`/`1`/`I`/`l`, no-collision across 1000 samples pinning the ~70-bit entropy claim, ≥ 2 distinct symbols per output as a degenerate-generator guard); `newInvestorToken()` (16-char length guard AND strict-greater-than-newSlug length pinning the "longer than the anon share slug so per-investor links don't collide" invariant from the module comment, alphabet-set membership across 200 samples, look-alike absence across 500 samples, no-collision across 1000 samples pinning the ~93-bit entropy claim, cross-family disjointness — no investor token collides with any of 500 score slugs since lengths differ); `ALPHABET` invariants (exactly 57 characters matching the module comment `12 chars * 57 alphabet ≈ 70 bits`, zero duplicate characters, excludes every declared look-alike, contains representative digit/upper/lower boundaries `2`/`9`/`A`/`Z`/`a`/`z`). Covers module constants (`MENTOR_ACCESS_TIERS` in canonical `attributed_only`→`reports_shared`→`full_mentor` order, `TIER_RANK` 0/1/2 strictly increasing per-tier, `CONSENT_LIFETIME_DAYS === 365` pinning the 12-month grant length, `EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS === [30, 7]` descending pinning the two consent-expiry-cron windows); pure ladder helpers `tierAtLeast` (null/undefined caller returns false, equal-tier true, higher-tier true, lower-tier false — all three cross-tier pairs) and `requireTier` (delegates identically — pins the alias contract so future refactors can't diverge them silently); copy resolvers `tierLabel` (bit-for-bit "Attributed only"/"Reports shared"/"Full mentor" pins so a rename in the settings-copy PR surfaces here, non-empty label for every tier in the enum), `tierBadgeColor` (attributed_only→default, reports_shared→brand, full_mentor→success, closed-set guard against invented shadcn variants), `tierDisclosure` (>20-char copy per tier, attributed_only mentions "masked"+"No SVI"+"cap-table", full_mentor is a strict superset mentioning "SVI evidence"+"cap-table"+"mentor notes"); grant-scoped predicates `canViewReport` (null grant → false, attributed_only → false even when founder toggled sharing on, reports_shared + `shared_with_mentor=true` → true, full_mentor + `shared_with_mentor=false` → false pinning the per-report-toggle-wins-over-tier contract, expired grant → false even at full_mentor, revoked grant → false), `canViewSviEvidence` (only full_mentor + active + not-revoked → true, attributed_only + reports_shared both → false, expired/revoked at full_mentor → false), `canLeaveNote` (mirrors canViewSviEvidence — only full_mentor active); time-scoped predicates `isExpiringSoon` (no grant / null expires_at → false pinning the attributed_only-never-expires carve-out, inclusive `<= days` boundary at exactly 30 days remaining → true, 31 days → false pinning the strict boundary, already-expired → false as a separate signal, revoked-within-window → false, default `days = 30` fallback), `isExpired` (no grant / null expires_at → false, `<=` boundary at expires_at === now → true, past → true, future → false), `isEffective` (missing grant → false, revoked → false, expired → false, active-within-window → true, no-expiry attributed_only grant → true); ladder invariants (canLeaveNote implies canViewSviEvidence for every tier in the enum, canViewSviEvidence implies `grant.tier === "full_mentor"` — pins the "no lower tier can bypass the SVI-evidence gate" contract). First test coverage for `mentor/access-tiers.ts` — pins the tier ladder + `canViewReport` per-report-toggle-wins-over-tier gate + boundary semantics on `isExpiringSoon`/`isExpired` so a silent rename of a tier id, a re-ordering of `MENTOR_ACCESS_TIERS`, a re-tuning of `CONSENT_LIFETIME_DAYS`, or an off-by-one on the expiring-soon inclusive boundary surfaces at unit-test time before it leaks a founder's SVI evidence or reports past a revoked / expired grant. **shipped 2026-07-31 (P9-segment-report-lib-test):** colocated 55-case vitest for the previously-untested pure `web/src/lib/retention/segment-report.ts` — the markdown formatter the `/api/cron/weekly-retention` route calls once per audience segment to render the retention snapshot email body (T-0418). Covers the shared report shape (level-1 title `# Retention snapshot — <segment>`, italic `_As of <asOf>_` line rendered verbatim from `SegmentMetrics.asOf` — no re-formatting so a caller-shipped ISO stamp round-trips bit-for-bit, 4-row metric table with the exact header + `| --- | --- |` alignment row, level-2 `## Summary` heading positioned after the table, trailing newline pinned, strict title→asOf→table→summary section ordering) plus number formatting (activeSubs + churn30d rendered via `toLocaleString("en-AU")` — 1,234,567 shape pinned; trialConversion rendered as percentage via `toFixed(1)` — 42.75% → "42.8%", 0.12345 → "12.3%" pinning the truncation-not-half-up posture; net revenue rendered as `A$` integer via `Math.round` + `toLocaleString("en-AU")` — 9876.51 → A$9,877, 0.49 → A$0, 0.5 → A$1 pinning the half-up boundary, negative revenue → `A$-12,345` pinning the refund-month contract). Default (unknown-segment) branch (arbitrary `"mentor"` → fallback prefix + "trial-to-paid conversion" phrase + churn/revenue echo; empty-string segment falls through to fallback with empty title suffix + `: `-prefixed summary pinning that the fallback path never crashes on a mis-spelled cron segment). Numeric edge cases in `formatPct` (NaN / ±Infinity / 0 / 1 all render — NaN + ±Infinity trigger the `Number.isFinite` fallback to "0.0%" so a divide-by-zero in the caller's active-subs → conversion ratio never leaks a "NaN%" into a customer-facing email) and `formatAud` (NaN / +Infinity trigger the fallback to "A$0" — same divide-by-zero guard for a caller's ARR calc; 0-value active + 0-value churn render as "0" not empty). First test coverage for `retention/segment-report.ts` — pins the 7-segment anchor phrases (so a settings-copy rename surfaces here before it lands in a founder's inbox), the `Number.isFinite` divide-by-zero guards (so a live caller-ratio NaN never emits "NaN%" or "A$NaN" into the weekly digest), and the strict section ordering (so a title/summary swap surfaces at unit-test time before it desynchronises the downstream markdown-to-HTML email renderer that keys off the level-1/level-2 heading positions).

      - E2E playwright spec proves 9-step logged-out walkthrough of /showcase/atlassian reaches Chapter 12 and all mapped BlockID surfaces exist

open_questions:
  Q1_safe_templates:
    q: Do we mint SAFE / convertible-note templates ourselves (LawPath-style) or link out to LawPath / CommercialAgreements?
    recommend: link out at Phase 6, mint AU-flavoured SAFE ourselves once P1_dataroom_map surfaces >2 requests
  Q2_wholesale_cert_flow:
    q: Is s708(8) wholesale-investor accountant-certificate collection in scope for the platform, or handled off-platform by the founder's lawyer?
    recommend: off-platform for MVP; expose an evidence slot at Phase 10 that lets founder upload the counter-signed cert; NEVER issue or validate the certificate ourselves (that is a s923B "financial services" boundary)
  Q3_valuation_disclaimer:
    q: Does BlockID's SVI-calibrated valuation output need an AFSL exemption note stronger than the current legal/surfaces.ts:49 wording?
    recommend: add a "general information only, not personal financial product advice per s766B Corps Act" line to any valuation PDF; keep valuation output as tools/analysis, not advice
  Q4_dual_class_capability:
    q: Do we surface Atlassian's dual-class share structure as an option? ASX prohibits dual-class; UK Plc or Delaware C-corp only.
    recommend: at Phase 9/10 add a decision-point doc "single-class default; dual-class needs offshore parent" with links to StartupDaily coverage; do NOT auto-generate offshore incorporation documents
  Q5_secondary_round_workflow:
    q: Atlassian's two pre-IPO rounds were 100% secondary (Accel 2010, T.Rowe 2014). Do we model secondary in Chapter 10?
    recommend: yes — extend Chapter 10 to distinguish primary vs secondary raise; secondary needs cap-table + Right-of-First-Refusal + s708 exemption checks
  Q6_pledge_1pct:
    q: Do we prompt founders to consider Pledge 1% (Atlassian co-founded it in 2006) at Phase 8?
    recommend: soft nudge only; opt-in, never opt-out; deep-link to pledge1percent.org
  Q7_shipit_hackathon:
    q: Do we surface ShipIt as a culture pattern in Chapter 8 (currently only in Atlassian showcase)?
    recommend: add to Chapter 8 sections[] as "Culture rituals — quarterly ShipIt inspiration"
  Q8_r_and_d_tax_incentive:
    q: R&D Tax Incentive is a huge AU-specific cashflow lever. Is it in the guide today?
    recommend: currently only referenced in data-room folder 11 "R&D Tax Incentive Claims" (data-room-templates.ts:1256); ADD a dedicated Chapter 6 section on R&D Tax Incentive eligibility + AusIndustry registration deadline (10 months after FY end)
  Q9_lp_report_bundle_scope:
    q: The lp_report bundling in Chapter 12 assumes reseller = accelerator. Do we support other reseller archetypes (angel syndicate, super fund co-investor)?
    recommend: keep accelerator MVP; extend to angel syndicate in P10+ (needs different anonymisation rules)
  Q10_atlassian_us_facts:
    q: Some Atlassian facts (Rich Wong joining board 2010, ~US$55M cash-on-hand pre-Accel) came from single TechCrunch sources. Do we citation-double-source before shipping copy?
    recommend: yes — every claim used in a founder-facing callout needs a second source; run a citation audit at P4
---
```

## 1. Phase gap matrix (Deliverable 1)

Legend: **Priority** — P0 = raises are blocked without it, P1 = raises are harder without it, P2 = nice to have.
Line references anchored to files current as of 2026-07-24; verify with `git blame` if drift is suspected.

| 1 | `01-vision` — Vision / Day-0 Idea (`showcase/gallery.ts:24`) | 2002 — Mike Cannon-Brookes + Scott Farquhar UNSW grads, A$10K credit card, explicit "don't wear suits, earn more than PwC's offer" (source: [Atlassian 20-year blog](https://www.atlassian.com/blog/announcements/atlassian-founders-20-years-20-lessons); `web/src/app/showcase/atlassian/page.tsx:37-43`) | Founder Agreement (informal ok), ABN application via ABR, `.com.au` domain reservation, initial IP-ownership statement (a "background IP" clause, not yet a full deed) | `/guide/01-vision` (`web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts:74-138`), `/svi` (workspace bootstrap), `/showcase/blockid` | **Missing:** live ABR ABN-lookup probe (no `/api/abr/lookup`); ## 2. Data-room auto-population plan (Deliverable 2)

**Note on count:** The spec said "60-item data room". The actual taxonomy in `web/src/lib/data-room-templates.ts:425-1443` currently has **102 items across 12 folders**. Mapping ≥ 90 of 102 per success_criteria.

**Atlassian S-1 reference:** filed 2015-11-25, effective 2015-12-08, IPO 2015-12-10. Exhibit index reachable via [SEC EDGAR CIK 0001650372](https://www.sec.gov/cgi-bin/browse-edgar?action=getcompany&CIK=0001650372&type=S-1). Exhibit numbers below follow the S-1 exhibit-index conventions (3.x = charter/bylaws, 4.x = securities-instruments, 10.x = material-contracts, 21.1 = subsidiaries, 23.1 = auditor consent, 5.1 = legality opinion).
